the first record is the header, later records are data records, and a quoted comma stays inside one value. |\n| INPUT-002 | Trim values and classify nulls | Header names and values are trimmed; empty, NA, N/A, and NULL ignoring case are null; all other values are non-null. |\n| INPUT-003 | Reject malformed files | An empty file, an empty or repeated header name, or a ragged record exits 3, writes nothing to standard output, and the diagnostic names the file and line. |\n| INPUT-004 | Report unreadable files | A file that cannot be opened or read exits 1 and writes nothing to standard output. |\n\n### Feature: Command Line\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Parse options and the file argument | Options precede the single required file; `--column`, `--freq`, and `--top` are accepted in the specified forms. |\n| CLI-002 | Profile one named column | `--column <name>` profiles only the named column; a name absent from the header exits 3. |\n| CLI-003 | Reject usage errors | A missing or extra file, unknown option, missing option value, invalid `--top`, `--top` without `--freq`, or `--column` with `--freq` exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output. |\n\n### Feature: Column Typing\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TYPE-001 | Infer integer, number, boolean, text, and empty | Each column takes the first matching type among those its non-null values all satisfy; a column with no non-null values is empty. |\n| TYPE-002 | Infer date columns with calendar validation | A column is date when every non-null value matches YYYY-MM-DD and is a real calendar date; `2025-02-30` is not a date. |\n\n### Feature: Column Profile\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| PROF-001 | Count rows, nulls, and distinct values | Each column reports rows, nulls, and distinct non-null values compared as exact trimmed strings, in file order. |\n| PROF-002 | Report min and max by type | Min and max follow the type rules, including byte-order text, boolean, date, and blank values for empty columns. |\n| PROF-003 | Report sum, mean, and median for numeric columns | Integer and number columns report sum, mean, and median; number and decimal statistics are rounded to two places, half away from zero; other types leave them empty. |\n\n### Feature: CSV Output\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| OUT-001 | Write profile and frequency output as CSV | Output uses `\\n` line endings with encoding/csv quoting; a failing command writes one stderr diagnostic and nothing to stdout. |\n\n### Feature: Value Frequencies\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FREQ-001 | List the most frequent values | With `--freq`, non-null values are listed by count descending then byte value ascending, at most `--top` rows (default 5); a column with no non-null values writes only the header. |\n\n### Feature: Build and Verification\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| BUILD-001 | Build csvstat from a standard-library Go module | The application root is a Go module from which `go build ./cmd/csvstat` produces the executable using only the standard library. |\n| BUILD-002 | Run vet and tests from one script | `bin/test.sh` (POSIX sh) runs `go vet ./...` and `go test ./...` from the application root and exits zero only when both pass. |\n| BUILD-003 | Pass the supplied conformance harness | `sh sources/run.sh` exits zero with every supplied check passing; this is the terminal verification story. |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | INPUT-001 | The file is not rejected for containing bytes that are not valid UTF-8; such bytes are read as encoding/csv reads them. |\n| AC-002 | CLI-003 | No help flag is defined: `--help` and `-h` are unknown options and exit 2. Every usage error writes both synopses, `csvstat [--column <name>] <file>` and `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`, to standard error. |\n| AC-003 | CLI-002 | `--column` and `--freq` names match trimmed header names by exact, case-sensitive comparison. |\n| AC-004 | PROF-003 | Sums, means, and medians are computed with exact arithmetic so that integer totals cannot overflow, and rounding applies to the exact value. |\n| AC-005 | BUILD-001 | The executable targets the host platform of the `go build` invocation and has no runtime dependency beyond the built binary. |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | instruction-to-test | sources/run.sh | INSTRUCTIONS \"Run Harness\" defines the 22 checks, exit-code and stdout comparison that run.sh implements | BUILD-003 proves the brief's contract by running run.sh. |\n| sources/run.sh | test-kit-to-implementation | ./cmd/csvstat | run.sh runs `go build -o <tmp> ./cmd/csvstat` and compares every command's exit code and stdout | BUILD-001 must produce the executable at the path run.sh builds. |\n| sources/run.sh | dependency | sources/employees.csv, sources/sensors.csv, sources/orders.csv, sources/header_only.csv, sources/ragged.csv | run.sh resolves `DATA` to the staged data directory and passes these paths to csvstat | The data files must be staged beside run.sh in `sources/`. |\n| sources/ragged.csv | fixture-to-story | INPUT-003 | Line 4 has one extra field; run.sh check E1 expects exit 3 | INPUT-003 is verified by E1. |\n| sources/header_only.csv | fixture-to-story | PROF-001, FREQ-001 | run.sh check A4 expects type `empty` with zero rows | TYPE-001 and PROF-001 are verified by A4. |\n| sources/orders.csv | fixture-to-story | TYPE-002, INPUT-002 | Padded customer text and the invalid date `2025-02-30` in `shipped` | TYPE-002 and INPUT-002 are verified by A3 and C3. |\n| sources/employees.csv | fixture-to-story | PROF-003, FREQ-001 | Quoted commas in `notes` and a `manager_id` null mix | PROF-003 and FREQ-001 are verified by A1, B1, B2, and C1, C4. |\n| sources/sensors.csv | fixture-to-story | TYPE-001, PROF-002 | Null spellings, an all-null `battery` column, and a `number` column | TYPE-001 and PROF-002 are verified by A2, B3, and C5. |\n\n## Source Roles\n\n| Path | Role | Plan disposition | Build disposition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | normative specification | context | prompt-only |\n| sources/run.sh | conformance harness | context | stage |\n| sources/employees.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/sensors.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/orders.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/header_only.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/ragged.csv | asset | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\ncsvstat is a CLI. Input: one CSV file path plus optional `--column`, `--freq`, and `--top` flags. Output: profile or frequency CSV on standard output, with one stderr diagnostic per failure. Exit codes are 0 success, 1 unreadable file, 2 usage error, 3 rejected input. Execution is single-pass: read, validate, infer types, then compute the requested profile or frequency list. There is no persistence, network use, or configuration file.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- INPUT-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Reading the file\" 1. Related: sources/employees.csv. Test: go test with files written by the test.\n- INPUT-002: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Reading the file\" 2 and 4. Related: sources/sensors.csv, sources/orders.csv.\n- INPUT-003: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Reading the file\" 3 and Exit codes 3. Related: sources/ragged.csv; run.sh E1.\n- INPUT-004: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS Exit codes 1; run.sh E4.\n- CLI-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Command\" and \"Options precede the file\"; run.sh U1, U2, U6.\n- CLI-002: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS Command table `--column`; run.sh B1 to B3, E2.\n- CLI-003: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Command\" constraints and Exit codes 2; run.sh U3 to U5.\n- TYPE-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Types\" rows for integer, number, boolean, text, and empty; related: sources/employees.csv, sources/sensors.csv.\n- TYPE-002: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Types\" date row; related: sources/orders.csv.\n- PROF-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Profile output\" for rows, nulls, and distinct; run.sh A1 to A4.\n- PROF-002: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Profile output\" min and max; run.sh A1 to A3.\n- PROF-003: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Profile output\" sum, mean, and median and the \"Decimals\" paragraph; run.sh A1 to A3.\n- OUT-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Output format\"; applies to every run.sh stdout comparison.\n- FREQ-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Frequency output\"; run.sh C1 to C5, E3.\n- BUILD-001: infrastructure. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Stack\" and the run.sh build step.\n- BUILD-002: test harness. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Tests\".\n- BUILD-003: acceptance contract. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Run Harness\" and sources/run.sh, all 22 checks.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\n- Focused story tests: each feature story runs `go test` for the slice it owns, with fixture files written by the test, and declares `Suite: scoped`. No feature story runs sources/run.sh.\n- Terminal verification: BUILD-003 is the only story that runs the full conformance harness. It depends on every implementation story, declares `Suite: full`, and runs `sh sources/run.sh`. run.sh accepts no scope selector, so no story runs it partially.\n- Sea Trial st-001 is the same full proof at project level.\n- BUILD-002 is proved by a scoped test that runs `bin/test.sh` against a temporary module, not by the supplied harness.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\n- BUILD-001 precedes every other story; BUILD-002 follows BUILD-001.\n- INPUT-001 precedes INPUT-002; INPUT-002 precedes INPUT-003, INPUT-004, PROF-001, and FREQ-001.\n- INPUT-002 precedes TYPE-001; TYPE-001 precedes TYPE-002, PROF-002, and PROF-003.\n- CLI-001 precedes CLI-002 and CLI-003.\n- PROF-001, PROF-002, PROF-003, and FREQ-001 precede OUT-001.\n- Fixtures are staged before any verification story runs.\n- BUILD-003 is last and depends on all implementation stories.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n- No cross-source conflicts. No `--help` flag. `--help` is an unknown option and exits 2 (AC-002).\n  2. Invalid UTF-8 is not a rejection condition, because the brief's rejection list is exhaustive (AC-001).\n  3. Integer and decimal totals use exact arithmetic (AC-004).\n  4. Column and frequency names match exactly after trimming (AC-003).\n  5. The two-decimal rule applies to integer `mean` and `median` as well as to number columns, as run.sh A1 shows (`1012.50`).\n\n## Analysis Notes\n\ngenerated: 2026-10-24\nblueprint: /mnt/c/Users/barlo/projects/drydock/uat/CsvStat/runs/20261024.203847/workspace/targets/CsvStat/blueprint\n\nQuality: Ready\n  blockers: 0\n  questions: 0\n  features: 7\n  stories: 17\n  stack: Go 1.22 or newer, standard library only\n  display_name: not proposed\n  short_description: not proposed\n\nObservations:\n- Project type: cli.\n- Unattended policy applied: no questionnaire or BLOCKERS.md was emitted. BEGIN ARTIFACT ANALYSIS.md ===\n# Blueprint Analysis: CsvStat\n\n## Commander Expectations\n\n- assert csvstat profiles a CSV file, reporting each column's type and the statistics the brief names\n- assert csvstat passes the supplied sources/run.sh grader without modification to the grader\n- assert csvstat uses only the Go standard library and no database\n\n## Crew\n\n| Crew | Charge |\n|---|---|\n| Commander | Defines intent and decides what done means. |\n| Team Lead | Confirms epic completeness and stakeholder expectations. |\n| Planning Crew | Authors atomic specifications and the ordered Manifest. |\n| Shipyard Crew | Builds the tickets without synchronous Commander access. |\n\n## Story List\n\n### Feature: Input Reading\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| INPUT-001 | Read a CSV file | A UTF-8 RFC 4180 file is parsed with encoding/csv; the first record is the header, later records are data records, and a quoted comma stays inside one value. |\n| INPUT-002 | Trim values and classify nulls | Header names and values are trimmed; empty, NA, N/A, and NULL ignoring case are null; all other values are non-null. |\n| INPUT-003 | Reject malformed files | An empty file, an empty or repeated header name, or a ragged record exits 3, writes nothing to standard output, and the diagnostic names the file and line. |\n| INPUT-004 | Report unreadable files | A file that cannot be opened or read exits 1 and writes nothing to standard output. |\n\n### Feature: Command Line\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Parse options and the file argument | Options precede the single required file; `--column`, `--freq`, and `--top` are accepted in the specified forms. |\n| CLI-002 | Profile one named column | `--column <name>` profiles only the named column; a name absent from the header exits 3. |\n| CLI-003 | Reject usage errors | A missing or extra file, unknown option, missing option value, invalid `--top`, `--top` without `--freq`, or `--column` with `--freq` exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output. |\n\n### Feature: Column Typing\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TYPE-001 | Infer integer, number, boolean, text, and empty | Each column takes the first matching type among those its non-null values all satisfy; a column with no non-null values is empty. |\n| TYPE-002 | Infer date columns with calendar validation | A column is date when every non-null value matches YYYY-MM-DD and is a real calendar date; `2025-02-30` is not a date. |\n\n### Feature: Column Profile\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| PROF-001 | Count rows, nulls, and distinct values | Each column reports rows, nulls, and distinct non-null values compared as exact trimmed strings, in file order. |\n| PROF-002 | Report min and max by type | Min and max follow the type rules, including byte-order text, boolean, date, and blank values for empty columns. |\n| PROF-003 | Report sum, mean, and median for numeric columns | Integer and number columns report sum, mean, and median; number and decimal statistics are rounded to two places, half away from zero; other types leave them empty. |\n\n### Feature: CSV Output\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| OUT-001 | Write profile and frequency output as CSV | Output uses `\\n` line endings with encoding/csv quoting; a failing command writes one stderr diagnostic and nothing to stdout. |\n\n### Feature: Value Frequencies\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FREQ-001 | List the most frequent values | With `--freq`, non-null values are listed by count descending then byte value ascending, at most `--top` rows (default 5); a column with no non-null values writes only the header. |\n\n### Feature: Build and Verification\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| BUILD-001 | Build csvstat from a standard-library Go module | The application root is a Go module from which `go build ./cmd/csvstat` produces the executable using only the standard library. |\n| BUILD-002 | Run vet and tests from one script | `bin/test.sh` (POSIX sh) runs `go vet ./...` and `go test ./...` from the application root and exits zero only when both pass. |\n| BUILD-003 | Pass the supplied conformance harness | `sh sources/run.sh` exits zero with every supplied check passing; this is the terminal verification story. |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | INPUT-001 | The file is not rejected for containing bytes that are not valid UTF-8; such bytes are read as encoding/csv reads them. |\n| AC-002 | CLI-003 | No help flag is defined: `--help` and `-h` are unknown options and exit 2. Every usage error writes both synopses, `csvstat [--column <name>] <file>` and `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`, to standard error. |\n| AC-003 | CLI-002 | `--column` and `--freq` names match trimmed header names by exact, case-sensitive comparison. |\n| AC-004 | PROF-003 | Sums, means, and medians are computed with exact arithmetic so that integer totals cannot overflow, and rounding applies to the exact value. |\n| AC-005 | BUILD-001 | The executable targets the host platform of the `go build` invocation and has no runtime dependency beyond the built binary. |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | instruction-to-test | sources/run.sh | INSTRUCTIONS \"Run Harness\" defines the 22 checks, exit-code and stdout comparison that run.sh implements | BUILD-003 proves the brief's contract by running run.sh. |\n| sources/run.sh | test-kit-to-implementation | ./cmd/csvstat | run.sh runs `go build -o <tmp> ./cmd/csvstat` and compares every command's exit code and stdout | BUILD-001 must produce the executable at the path run.sh builds. |\n| sources/run.sh | dependency | sources/employees.csv, sources/sensors.csv, sources/orders.csv, sources/header_only.csv, sources/ragged.csv | run.sh resolves `DATA` to the staged data directory and passes these paths to csvstat | The data files must be staged beside run.sh in `sources/`. |\n| sources/ragged.csv | fixture-to-story | INPUT-003 | Line 4 has one extra field; run.sh check E1 expects exit 3 | INPUT-003 is verified by E1. |\n| sources/header_only.csv | fixture-to-story | PROF-001, FREQ-001 | run.sh check A4 expects type `empty` with zero rows | TYPE-001 and PROF-001 are verified by A4. |\n| sources/orders.csv | fixture-to-story | TYPE-002, INPUT-002 | Padded customer text and the invalid date `2025-02-30` in `shipped` | TYPE-002 and INPUT-002 are verified by A3 and C3. |\n| sources/employees.csv | fixture-to-story | PROF-003, FREQ-001 | Quoted commas in `notes` and a `manager_id` null mix | PROF-003 and FREQ-001 are verified by A1, B1, B2, and C1, C4. |\n| sources/sensors.csv | fixture-to-story | TYPE-001, PROF-002 | Null spellings, an all-null `battery` column, and a `number` column | TYPE-001 and PROF-002 are verified by A2, B3, and C5. |\n\n## Source Roles\n\n| Path | Role | Plan disposition | Build disposition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | normative specification | context | prompt-only |\n| sources/run.sh | conformance harness | context | stage |\n| sources/employees.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/sensors.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/orders.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/header_only.csv | asset | context | stage |\n| sources/ragged.csv | asset | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\ncsvstat is a CLI. Input: one CSV file path plus optional `--column`, `--freq`, and `--top` flags. Output: profile or frequency CSV on standard output, with one stderr diagnostic per failure. Exit codes are 0 success, 1 unreadable file, 2 usage error, 3 rejected input. Execution is single-pass: read, validate, infer types, then compute the requested profile or frequency list. There is no persistence, network use, or configuration file.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- INPUT-001: capability. Cites INSTRUCTIONS \"Reading the file\" 1. Related: sources/employees.csv.
